<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-9E331C33-7AA2-4194-A24C-1B155799E154'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Circle the factors of $99$: 


$
\begin{matrix}
9   & \quad 198 & \quad 990 & \quad 33 \\
66  & \quad 11  & \quad 18  & \quad 48
\end{matrix}
$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{matrix}
\boxed{\green
{9}}   & \quad 198                            & \quad 990    & \boxed{\green
{33}} \\
66                                   & \boxed{\green
{11}}  & \quad 18     & \quad 48                           
\end{matrix}
$



Let's verify

$
\begin{aligned}
\underline 9 \times 11 &= 99 \\\\
\underline 11 \times 9 &= 99 \\\\
\underline 33 \times 3 &= 99
\end{aligned}
$



</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$9, 11, 33$


</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B4B7A00B-273C-468F-A81C-B5CBD794AFE8'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Subtract $20 + (1 \times 7)$ from $(20 + 1) \times 7$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Using BIDMAS
,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= ((20 + 1) \times 7) - (20 + (1 \times 7)) \\\\
&= (21 \times 7) - (20 + 7) \\\\
&= 147 - 27 \\\\
&= 120
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$120$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_bidmas,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
&= ((20 + 1) \times 7) - (20 + (1 \times 7)) \\\\
&= (21 \times 7) - (20 + 7) \\\\
&= 147 - 27 \\\\
&= 120
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$120$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Calculate $0.35 + \dfrac{4}{5} + \dfrac{37}{100}$, leaving your answer as a decimal. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 0.35 + \dfrac {4} {5} + \dfrac {37} {100} \\\\
&= 0.35 + \dfrac {4 \times 2} {5 \times 2} + \dfrac {37} {100} \\\\
&= 0.35+ \dfrac {8} {10} + \dfrac {37} {100} \\\\
&= 0.35 + 0.8 + 0.37 \\\\
&= 1.52
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1.52$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 0.35 + \dfrac {4} {5} + \dfrac {37} {100} \\\\
&= 0.35 + \dfrac {4 \times 2} {5 \times 2} + \dfrac {37} {100} \\\\
&= 0.35+ \dfrac {8} {10} + \dfrac {37} {100} \\\\
&= 0.35 + 0.8 + 0.37 \\\\
&= 1.52
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question subquestion'>

Write $\dfrac{21}{70}$ as a decimal. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= \dfrac {21} {70} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 7} {10 \times 7} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {7}} {10 \times \cancel {7}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {10} \\\\
&= 0.3
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.3$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$

&= \dfrac {21} {70} \\\\

&= \dfrac {3 \times \cancel {7}} {10 \times \cancel {7}} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3} {10} \\\\
&= 0.3
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-AF9BB69B-87C1-4385-B4A4-EC5CA1ED0C45'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Simon and Fiona are sharing a cupboard. If Simon’s things take up $\dfrac{2}{5}$ of the space and Fiona’s things take up 
$\dfrac{2}{7}$ of the space, what fraction of the cupboard is empty? 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Empty Space}      &= \text{Total space} - \text{Used space} \\\\
                        &= 1 - \bigg(\dfrac {2} {5} + \dfrac {2} {7}\bigg) \\\\
                        &= 1 - \bigg(\dfrac {2 \times 7} {5 \times 7} + \dfrac {2 \times 5} {7 \times 5}\bigg) \\\\
                        &= 1 - \bigg(\dfrac {14} {35} + \dfrac {10} {35}\bigg) \\\\
                        &= 1 - \bigg(\dfrac {14 + 10} {35}\bigg) \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1} {1} - \dfrac {24} {35} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1 \times 35} {1 \times 35} - \dfrac {24} {35} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {35 - 24} {35} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {11} {35}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac {11} {35}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Empty Space}      &= \text{Total space} - \text{Used space} \\\\
                        &= 1 - \bigg(\dfrac {2} {5} + \dfrac {2} {7}\bigg) \\\\
                        &= 1 - \bigg(\dfrac {2 \times 7} {5 \times 7} + \dfrac {2 \times 5} {7 \times 5}\bigg) \\\\
                        &= 1 - \bigg(\dfrac {14} {35} + \dfrac {10} {35}\bigg) \\\\
                        &= 1 - \bigg(\dfrac {14 + 10} {35}\bigg) \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1} {1} - \dfrac {24} {35} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1 \times 35} {1 \times 35} - \dfrac {24} {35} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {35 - 24} {35} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {11} {35}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac {11} {35}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

The side length of each small square in the grid is $1 \ \text{cm}$. Work out the area of the quadrilateral $ABCD$. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Height of the trapezium}          &= 5 - 1 \\\\
                                        &= 4 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Length of one parallel side}      &= 4 - 1 \\\\
                                        &= 3 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Length of another parallel side}  &= 3 - 1 \\\\
                                        &= 2 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Area of the trapezium}            &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \text{Sum of parallel sides} \times \text{Height of the trapezium} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (3 + 2) \times 4 \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 5 \times 4 \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {5 \times 4} {2} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {5 \times 2 \times 2} {2} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {5 \times 2 \times \cancel 2} {\cancel 2} \\\\
                                        &= 5 \times 2 \\\\
                                        &= 10 \ {\text{cm}} ^ 2
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$10 \ {\text{cm}} ^ 2$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Height of the trapezium}          &= 5 - 1 \\\\
                                        &= 4 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Length of one parallel side}      &= 4 - 1 \\\\
                                        &= 3 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Length of another parallel side}  &= 3 - 1 \\\\
                                        &= 2 \ \text{cm} \\\\
\text{Area of the trapezium}            &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times \text{Sum of parallel sides} \times \text{Height of the trapezium} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times (3 + 2) \times 4 \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {1} {2} \times 5 \times 4 \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {5 \times 4} {2} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {5 \times 2 \times 2} {2} \\\\
                                        &= \dfrac {5 \times 2 \times \cancel 2} {\cancel 2} \\\\
                                        &= 5 \times 2 \\\\
                                        &= 10 \ {\text{cm}} ^ 2
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>

<div class='answercodeblock'>

</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$10 \ {\text{cm}} ^ 2$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

Work out the perimeter of this shape. 

Note: all angles are right angles but the diagram has not been drawn to scale. 

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the diagram,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Perimeter}   &= \text {Sum of all sides} \\\\ 
                    &= 17 + 11 + 14 + 3 + 6 + 7 \\\\
                    &= 58 \ \text {cm}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

$58 \ \text {cm}$

</div>
</div>

<div class='question subquestion'>

$17^2 - 13^2$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 17^2 - 13^2 \\\\
&= (17 + 13) (17 - 13) \\\\
&= 30 \times 4 \\\\
&= 120
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$120$

</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-CF68CF7E-AF94-4947-B7CC-2F6917DF45D1'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The mean (average) of five numbers is $28$. The mean of a different set of twelve numbers is $11$. 
Calculate the mean of all of the numbers together. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$


As per the question,

$
\begin {aligned}
\text{Mean}                     &= \dfrac {5 \times 28 + 12 \times 11} {5 + 12} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {140 + 132} {17} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {272} {17} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {16 \times 17} {17} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {16 \times \cancel {17}} {\cancel {17}} \\\\
                                &= 16
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$16$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_statistics_formula_mean
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin {aligned}
\text{Mean}                     &= \dfrac {5 \times 28 + 12 \times 11} {5 + 12} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {140 + 132} {17} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {272} {17} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {16 \times 17} {17} \\\\
                                &= \dfrac {16 \times \cancel {17}} {\cancel {17}} \\\\
                                &= 16
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$16$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-FAE2F782-518B-4206-8667-3503E5D88609'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Work out the value of $x$ from the diagram below. 

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: $\text{Angles on a straight line add up to} = 180 \degree$


ABBR: $\text{Sum of angles in a triangle} = 180 \degree$


*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing starts.*

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)



*TODO:: Add a triangle with vertices ABC and a middle point as D. Mark angle BAD as a and angle DAC as b*


*ABBR: TODO:: Add drawing ends.*


As per the diagram,

$
\begin {aligned}
b + 70                  &= 180 \\\\
b                       &= 180 - 70 \\\\
b                       &= 110 \degree
\end {aligned}
$

Let's use $b$ to find the value of $a$.

As per the diagram, in triangle $ABC$

$
\begin {aligned}
a + a + b               &= 180 \\\\
2a + 110                &= 180 \\\\
2a                      &= 180 - 110 \\\\
2a                      &= 70 \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {70} {2} \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {2 \times 35} {2} \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac {\cancel {2} \times 35} {\cancel {2}} \\\\
a                       &= 35 \degree
\end {aligned}
$

Let's use the value of $a$ to find $x$.

As per the diagram, in triangle $ABD$,

$
\begin {aligned}
x + a + a   &= 180 \\\\
x + 35 + 35 &= 180 \\\\
x + 70      &= 180 \\\\
x           &= 180 - 70 \\\\
x           &= 110 \degree
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$110 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

The shape below is made up of equilateral triangles. The side length of the largest triangle is $16 \ \text{cm}$. 
Work out the total length of all of the lines used to draw the shape. 

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)


</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

$
</code></pre>

<div class='answercodeblock'>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-B2A70184-F3DC-4A6A-AC4D-2F3D1B344BA6'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Jon has $30\%$ more money than Miguel and together they have $\pounds 46$. Calculate how much money Jon has. 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let Miguel has (in $\pounds$) $= a$

Jon has (in $\pounds$) $= a + 0.3 \times a = 1.3a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
a + 1.3a                        &= 46 \\\\
2.3a                            &= 46 \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {46} {2.3} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {46 \times 10} {23} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {23 \times 2 \times 10} {23} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {\cancel{23} \times 2 \times 10} {\cancel{23}} \\\\
a                               &= 2 \times 10 \\\\
a                               &= \pounds 20 \\\\
\text {Jon has}                 &= 1.3 \times a \\\\
                                &= 1.3 \times 20 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 26
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 26$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let Miguel has (in $\pounds$) $= a$
<emptyline>
Jon has (in $\pounds$) $= a + 0.3 \times a = 1.3a$
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
a + 1.3a                        &= 46 \\\\
2.3a                            &= 46 \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {46} {2.3} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {46 \times 10} {23} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {23 \times 2 \times 10} {23} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {\cancel{23} \times 2 \times 10} {\cancel{23}} \\\\
a                               &= 2 \times 10 \\\\
a                               &= \pounds 20 \\\\
\text {Jon has}                 &= 1.3 \times a \\\\
                                &= 1.3 \times 20 \\\\
                                &= \pounds 26
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 26$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

A group of children are playing a game where they can score either $2, 3$ or $5$ points on each turn. 
The children have had $8$ turns each and recorded their scores in a table, but some of the entries have been deleted. 
Fill in the missing numbers to give a complete set of scores.

| **Name**    | **2 points** | **3 points** | **5 points** | **Total Score** |
|:-----------:|:------------:|:------------:|:------------:|:---------------:|
| Carl        |  1           |    6         |    1         |    25           |
| Alex        |  4           |    2         |              |                 |
| Yusuf       |              |    3         |              |    25           |
| Jing        |              |              |              |    35           |
| Charlotte   |              |              |              |    18           |

</div>

<div class='question question'>

Write each of the numbers $80, 81, 82, 83, 84$ and $85$ in the spaces below, using each number only once, to make all of the statements true.


$\text{\textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore } \qquad$
is a square number

$\text{\textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore } \qquad$ 
is a prime number

$\text{\textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore } \qquad$
is a multiple of $5$

$\text{\textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore } \qquad$
is the product of two prime numbers

$\text{\textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore } \qquad$
is double a prime number

$\text{\textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore \textunderscore } \qquad$
is a multiple of $3$  

</div>

<div class='question subquestion'>

Work out the value of $14 \ \spadesuit \ 55$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
&= 14 \ \spadesuit \ 55 \\\\
&= 14 \times 2 + 55 \\\\
&= 28 + 55 \\\\
&= 83
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>

</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is the value of $a$ if $9 \ \spadesuit \ a = 31$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
9 \ \spadesuit \ a &= 31 \\\\
9 \times 2 + a     &= 31 \\\\
18 + a             &= 31 \\\\
a                  &= 31 - 18 \\\\
a                  &= 13
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$13$

</div>
</div>

a                  &= 31 - 18 \\\\
a                  &= 13
\end {aligned}
$

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

What is the value of $b$ if $b \ \spadesuit \ 10 = 16 \ \spadesuit \ b$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
b \ \spadesuit \ 10 &= 16 \ \spadesuit \ b \\\\
b \times 2 + 10     &= 16 \times 2 + b \\\\
2b + 10             &= 32 + b \\\\
2b - b              &= 32 - 10 \\\\
b                   &= 22
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$22$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

b \ \spadesuit \ 10 &= 16 \ \spadesuit \ b \\\\
b \times 2 + 10     &= 16 \times 2 + b \\\\
2b + 10             &= 32 + b \\\\
2b - b              &= 32 - 10 \\\\
b                   &= 22
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>

The numbers $c$ and $d$ are such that $c \ \spadesuit \ d = d \ \spadesuit \ c$. Work out the value of $d - c$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin {aligned}
c \ \spadesuit \ d &= d \ \spadesuit \ c \\\\
c \times 2 + d     &= d \times 2 + c \\\\
2c + d             &= 2d + c \\\\
2c - c             &= 2d - d \\\\
c                  &= d \\\\
d - c              &= 0
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0$

2c + d             &= 2d + c \\\\
2c - c             &= 2d - d \\\\
c                  &= d \\\\
d - c              &= 0
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-DBB8AAA3-8E4B-4E8E-B062-598C9668FDFC'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The cards below have letters on one side, and the numbers $1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6$ and $7$ on the other side (but not necessarily in this order on the cards). 

$
\boxed{\text{P}} \qquad
\boxed{\text{H}} \qquad
\boxed{\text{A}} \qquad
\boxed{\text{B}} \qquad
\boxed{\text{L}} \qquad
\boxed{\text{E}} \qquad
\boxed{\text{T}} 
$

You are told that:

$
\boxed{\text{A}} \quad
\boxed{\text{L}} \quad
\boxed{\text{P}} \quad
\boxed{\text{H}} \quad
\boxed{\text{A}} \quad
\boxed{\text{B}} \quad
\boxed{\text{E}} \quad
\boxed{\text{T}} 
$  adds up to $35$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working placeholder'>

TODOWORKING

</div>
<div class='working placeholder'>

TODOWORKING

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer placeholder'>

</div>
<div class='answer placeholder'>

</div>
</div>

<ul class='subquestion lower-alpha'>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Find the value of $\boxed{\text{A}}$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the numbers on cards $= p, \ h, \ a, \ b, \ l, \ e, \ t$ 

As per the question,

$
\begin {aligned}
a + l + p + h + a + b + e + t &= 35 \\\\
1 + 2 + 3 + 4 + 5 + 6 + 7 + a &= 35 \\\\
28 + a                        &= 35 \\\\
a                             &= 35 - 28 \\\\
a                             &= 7 
\end {aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$7$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let the numbers on cards $= p, \ h, \ a, \ b, \ l, \ e, \ t$ 
<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin {aligned}
a + l + p + h + a + b + e + t &= 35 \\\\
1 + 2 + 3 + 4 + 5 + 6 + 7 + a &= 35 \\\\
28 + a                        &= 35 \\\\
a                             &= 35 - 28 \\\\
a                             &= 7 
\end {aligned}
$
<emptyline>
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$7$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

You are also told that:

$
\boxed{\text{A}} \quad
\boxed{\text{L}} \quad
\boxed{\text{P}} \quad
\boxed{\text{H}} \quad
\boxed{\text{A}} 
$ adds up to $20$. 

Work out what the following add up to: 

$
\boxed{\text{B}} \quad
\boxed{\text{E}} \quad
\boxed{\text{T}} \quad 
\boxed{\text{A}}
$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

As per the question,

$
\begin {aligned}
a + l + p + h + a + b + e + t &= 35 \\\\
20 + b + e + t                &= 35 \\\\
b + e + t                     &= 35 - 20 \\\\
b + e + t                     &= 15 \\\\
b + e + t + a                 &= 15 + a \\\\
b + e + t + a                 &= 15 + 7 \\\\
b + e + t + a                 &= 22
\end {aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$22$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin {aligned}
a + l + p + h + a + b + e + t &= 35 \\\\
20 + b + e + t                &= 35 \\\\
b + e + t                     &= 35 - 20 \\\\
b + e + t                     &= 15 \\\\
b + e + t + a                 &= 15 + a \\\\
b + e + t + a                 &= 15 + 7 \\\\
b + e + t + a                 &= 22
\end {aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$22$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>
</li>
</ul>

<div class='question question'>

Each of the diagrams below shows a pair of triangles drawn on a grid of squares of side length $1 \ \text{cm}$ and a smaller shaded triangle 
where these overlap. Each corner of each of the large triangles lies exactly at the corner of a square on the grid.
Work out the area of the shaded triangle in each case.
